
### Tool call: browser_resize
- Args
```json
{
  "width": 1440,
  "height": 1000
}
```
- Result
```json
{
  "code": "await page.setViewportSize({ width: 1440, height: 1000 });",
  "page": "- Page URL: about:blank"
}
```

### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "async () => {await navigator.serviceWorker.ready; return {browser:navigator.userAgent,serviceWorkerReady:true};}"
}
```
- Result
```json
{
  "result": "{\n  \"browser\": \"Mozilla/5.0 (X11; Linux x86_64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) HeadlessChrome/157.0.0.0 Safari/537.36\",\n  \"serviceWorkerReady\": true\n}",
  "code": "await page.evaluate('async () => {await navigator.serviceWorker.ready; return {browser:navigator.userAgent,serviceWorkerReady:true};}');"
}
```

No real carrier compatibility or certification is asserted.\n      - paragraph [ref=e1187]: Start the optional profile pack before configuring offers.\n      - generic [ref=e1188]:\n        - paragraph [ref=e1189]: Profiles remain inactive until observed ready and the next synthetic minute advances.\n        - button \"Start media profiles\" [ref=e1190] [cursor=pointer]\n    - generic [ref=e1191]:\n      - heading \"Managed operations\" [level=2] [ref=e1192]\n      - paragraph [ref=e1193]: Coordinate synthetic joiner and mover work across active customers. Completion and charges wait for effective resource readiness.\n      - paragraph [ref=e1194]: Start the customer portfolio before submitting managed work.\n    - generic [ref=e1195]:\n      - heading \"Site operations\" [level=2] [ref=e1196]\n      - paragraph [ref=e1197]: Bind real resources and transport sites before testing continuity. Each exercise checks a baseline call, continued service during a fault, and restoration from a captured configuration backup.\n      - paragraph [ref=e1198]: Start transport operations and provision transport sites first.\n    - generic [ref=e1199]:\n      - heading \"Specialist product lifecycle evidence\" [level=2] [ref=e1200]\n      - paragraph [ref=e1201]: Track scenario-only sale, renewal and support evidence against an effective product version and its current support owner.\n      - paragraph [ref=e1202]: Scenario evidence only; this does not establish real product availability, certification or vendor support.\n      - button \"Start lifecycle evidence\" [disabled] [ref=e1203]\n    - generic [ref=e1204]:\n      - heading \"Shared line and call controls\" [level=2] [ref=e1205]\n      - paragraph [ref=e1206]: Configure fictional shared-line appearances, presence and park/retrieve controls. These rules do not place real calls.\n      - paragraph [ref=e1207]: Shared lines, presence, pickup and park/retrieve are design-level decompositions. They do not establish complete source mapping or vendor parity.\n      - button \"Start shared services\" [disabled] [ref=e1209]\n      - paragraph [ref=e1210]: Start the calling directory and provision canonical identities first.\n    - generic [ref=e1211]:\n      - heading \"Admission and abuse controls\" [level=2] [ref=e1212]\n      - paragraph [ref=e1213]: Fictional controls for simulated packet admission, SIP signalling, and destination spend. They do not send network traffic.\n      - button \"Start security controls\" [ref=e1215] [cursor=pointer]\n    - generic [ref=e1216]:\n      - heading \"Sustained service observation\" [level=2] [ref=e1217]\n      - paragraph [ref=e1218]: Minute-sampled training evidence from real synthetic customer journeys, staffing, meetings and service paths.\n      - group [ref=e1219]:\n        - generic \"Source boundaries\" [ref=e1220] [cursor=pointer]\n      - generic [ref=e1221]:\n        - button \"Start observation pack\" [ref=e1222] [cursor=pointer]\n        - article [ref=e1223]:\n          - heading \"Transport continuity (rd)\" [level=3] [ref=e1224]\n          - paragraph [ref=e1225]:\n            - text: \"Status:\"\n            - strong [ref=e1226]: Not started\n          - group [ref=e1227]:\n            - generic \"Selected fictional thresholds\" [ref=e1228] [cursor=pointer]\n          - group [ref=e1229]:\n            - generic \"Guided setup\" [ref=e1230] [cursor=pointer]\n            - paragraph [ref=e1231]: Create two independent transport paths and resolve a real two-way human voice journey to the service queue before opening. Each exercise checks a baseline call, continued service during a fault, and restoration from a captured configuration backup.\n      - paragraph [ref=e3434]: Start transport operations and provision transport sites first.\n    - generic [ref=e3435]:\n      - heading \"Specialist product lifecycle evidence\" [level=2] [ref=e3436]\n      - paragraph [ref=e3437]: Track scenario-only sale, renewal and support evidence against an effective product version and its current support owner.\n      - paragraph [ref=e3438]: Scenario evidence only; this does not establish real product availability, certification or vendor support.\n      - button \"Start lifecycle evidence\" [disabled] [ref=e3439]\n    - generic [ref=e3440]:\n      - heading \"Shared line and call controls\" [level=2] [ref=e3441]\n      - paragraph [ref=e3442]: Configure fictional shared-line appearances, presence and park/retrieve controls.
